local ENV = (getfenv and getfenv(1)) or _ENV or _G
local Functions = {}
local unpackValues = ENV.table.unpack or ENV.unpack
local selectValues, toNumber = ENV.select, ENV.tonumber
local typeOf, getMetatable, rawGet, nextEntry = ENV.type, ENV.getmetatable, ENV.rawget, ENV.next
local fail = ENV.error

local function bind(fn, captures)
    return function(...) return fn(captures, ...) end
end

local function prepareIterator(value, state, key)
    if typeOf(value) == "function" then return value, state, key end
    local mt = getMetatable(value)
    if mt ~= nil and typeOf(mt) ~= "table" then
        fail("Protected iterator metatables require an explicit iterator function", 0)
    end
    local iterator = mt and rawGet(mt, "__iter")
    if iterator ~= nil then return iterator(value) end
    if typeOf(value) == "table" and (mt == nil or rawGet(mt, "__call") == nil) then
        return nextEntry, value, nil
    end
    return value, state, key
end

local function numericBound(value)
    local number = toNumber(value)
    if number == nil then fail("Numeric for bounds and step must be numbers", 0) end
    return number
end

local function appendValues(target, offset, ...)
    for index = 1, selectValues("#", ...) do
        target[offset + index - 1] = selectValues(index, ...)
    end
end

Functions.removeOldWindUI = function(captures)
    local value0, value0_2, name, value0_3, value0_4
    value0 = captures.upvalue1[1]
    value0_2 = value0.IsA(value0, "ScreenGui")
    if value0_2 then
        name = captures.upvalue1[1].Name
        value0_3 = name.find(name, "WindUI")
    else
        value0_3 = value0_2
    end
    if value0_3 then
        value0_4 = captures.upvalue1[1]
        value0_4.Destroy(value0_4)
    end
end

Functions.shutdownPreviousInstance = function(captures)
    ENV.pcall(captures.upvalue1[1], "replaced by new instance")
    captures.upvalue2[1] = true
end

        captures.files[1].makeFolder("NergBlock")
    end
end

Functions.getSetting = function(captures, key, default)
    local value2
    value2 = captures.settings[1]._data[key]
    if value2 ~= nil then
        return value2
    end
    return default
end

Functions.setSetting = function(captures, key, value)
    captures.settings[1]._data[key] = value
    captures.settings[1].save()
end

Functions.loadWindUI = function(captures)
    local value0, value1, loadstring, game
    value0, value1 = ENV.pcall(bind(Functions.loadPinnedWindUI, {}))
    if not (value0 and value1) then
        loadstring = ENV.loadstring
        game = ENV.game
        return loadstring(game.HttpGet(game, "https://github.com/Footagesus/WindUI/releases/latest/download/main.lua"))()
    end
    return value1
end


Functions.loadPinnedWindUI = function(captures)
    local loadstring, game
    loadstring = ENV.loadstring
    game = ENV.game
    return loadstring(game.HttpGet(game, "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Footagesus/WindUI/7dd8a34a6bb59635c7b5f18ce9d46558a8cde138/dist/main.lua"))()
end

    })
end

Functions.lockMovement = function(captures, a0)
    if not captures.movementLocked[1] then
        captures.movementLocked[1] = true
        return
    end
end


Functions.restoreMovement = function(captures, a0)
    local value0
    value0 = {a0}
    if captures.movementLocked[1] then
        captures.movementLocked[1] = false
        if value0[1] and value0[1].Parent then
            value0[1].AutoRotate = true
        end
        ENV.pcall(bind(Functions.applyMovementLock, {upvalue1 = value0}))
        return
    end
end


Functions.applyMovementLock = function(captures)
    local currentCamera, value1, value2, lookVector, value4
    currentCamera = ENV.workspace.CurrentCamera
    value1 = captures.upvalue1[1] and captures.upvalue1[1].Parent
    value2 = value1 and value1.FindFirstChild(value1, "HumanoidRootPart")
    if currentCamera and value2 then
        lookVector = currentCamera.CFrame.LookVector
        value4 = ENV.Vector3.new(lookVector.X, 0, lookVector.Z)
        if value4.Magnitude > 0.01 then
            value2.CFrame = ENV.CFrame.new(value2.Position, value2.Position + value4.Unit)
        end
    end
end


Functions.getLocalRoot = function(captures)
    local character
    character = captures.localPlayer[1].Character
    return character and character.FindFirstChild(character, "HumanoidRootPart")
end


Functions.sendBlock = function(captures)
    local value0
    if captures.remoteEvent[1] then
        value0 = captures.remoteEvent[1]
        value0.FireServer(value0, "UseActorAbility", {(ENV.buffer.fromstring("\003\005\000\000\000Block"))})
        return
    end
end


Functions.showCinematicText = function(captures)
    local value0, screenGui, textLabel
    if captures.cinematicTextEnabled[1] then
        if captures.upvalue2[1] then
            value0 = captures.upvalue2[1]
            value0.Destroy(value0)
            captures.upvalue2[1] = nil
        end
        screenGui = ENV.Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        screenGui.Name = "CinematicText"
        screenGui.Parent = captures.playerGui[1]
        screenGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
        screenGui.ZIndexBehavior = ENV.Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
        screenGui.DisplayOrder = 10000
        textLabel = ENV.Instance.new("TextLabel")
        textLabel.Size = ENV.UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 100)
        textLabel.Position = ENV.UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.4, -50)
        textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        textLabel.Text = captures.cinematicText[1]
        textLabel.TextColor3 = ENV.Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        textLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = ENV.Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        textLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.3
        textLabel.TextSize = 60
        textLabel.Font = ENV.Enum.Font.GothamBold
        textLabel.TextScaled = true
        textLabel.RichText = true
        textLabel.Parent = screenGui
        captures.upvalue2[1] = screenGui
        ENV.task.delay(1.5, bind(Functions.clearCinematicText, {upvalue1 = captures.upvalue2}))
        return
    end
end


Functions.clearCinematicText = function(captures)
    local value0
    if captures.upvalue1[1] then
        value0 = captures.upvalue1[1]
        value0.Destroy(value0)
        captures.upvalue1[1] = nil
    end
end


Functions.stopPunchAim = function(captures)
    local value0, value0_2
    if captures.punchAiming[1] then
        captures.punchAiming[1] = false
        captures.restoreMovement[1](captures.localHumanoid[1])
        if ENV.walkSpeed3 and captures.localHumanoid[1] then
            value0 = captures.localHumanoid[1]
            value0.WalkSpeed = ENV.walkSpeed3
            value0_2 = captures.localHumanoid[1]
            value0_2.JumpPower = ENV.jumpPower
            ENV.walkSpeed3 = nil
            ENV.jumpPower = nil
        end
        return
    end
end


Functions.attachLocalCharacter = function(captures, character)
    local findFirstChild, value2, value2_2, value2_3, animator, animationPlayed
    findFirstChild = character.FindFirstChild
    captures.localHumanoid[1] = character.FindFirstChild(character, "Humanoid")
    captures.localRoot[1] = findFirstChild(character, "HumanoidRootPart")
    if captures.localAnimationConnection[1] then
        value2 = captures.localAnimationConnection[1]
        value2.Disconnect(value2)
        captures.localAnimationConnection[1] = nil
    end
    value2_2 = captures.localHumanoid[1]
    if value2_2 then
        value2_3 = captures.localHumanoid[1]
        animator = value2_3.FindFirstChildOfClass(value2_3, "Animator")
    else
        animator = value2_2
    end
    if animator and captures.aimPunchActive[1] then
        animationPlayed = animator.AnimationPlayed
        captures.localAnimationConnection[1] = animationPlayed.Connect(animationPlayed, bind(Functions.onLocalAnimation, {
            aimPunchActive = captures.aimPunchActive,
            punchAnimationIds = captures.punchAnimationIds,
            punchAimStartedAt = captures.punchAimStartedAt,
            punchAiming = captures.punchAiming,
            stopPunchAim = captures.stopPunchAim,
        }))
    end

    end
    return nil
end

Functions.isBlockReady = function(captures)
    local value0, mainUI, abilityContainer, block, cooldownTime, text, value4, value5
    if not captures.noHDTCooldown[1] then
        value0 = captures.playerGui[1]
        mainUI = value0.FindFirstChild(value0, "MainUI")
        if not mainUI then
            return ENV.tick() - captures.lastBlockTime[1] >= captures.blockCooldown[1]
        end
        abilityContainer = mainUI.FindFirstChild(mainUI, "AbilityContainer")
        if not abilityContainer then
            return ENV.tick() - captures.lastBlockTime[1] >= captures.blockCooldown[1]
        end
        block = abilityContainer.FindFirstChild(abilityContainer, "Block")
        if not block then
            return ENV.tick() - captures.lastBlockTime[1] >= captures.blockCooldown[1]
        end
        cooldownTime = block.FindFirstChild(block, "CooldownTime")
        if not (cooldownTime and cooldownTime.IsA(cooldownTime, "TextLabel")) then
            return ENV.tick() - captures.lastBlockTime[1] >= captures.blockCooldown[1]
        end
        text = cooldownTime.Text
        value4 = text.match(text, "[%d%.]+")
        if not value4 then
            return true
        end
        value5 = ENV.tonumber(value4)
        return not value5 or value5 <= 0
    end
    return true
end

Functions.stepBodyPull = function(captures)
    local value0, humanoidRootPart, value1, value2, value3, unit, value4, value5, value5_2, value0_2
    if not (not captures.bodyPullEnabled[1] or not captures.bodyPullActive[1] or not (captures.character[1] and captures.character[1].Parent and captures.upvalue4[1] and captures.upvalue4[1].Parent) or captures.upvalue5[1] and captures.upvalue5[1].Health <= 0) then
        value0 = captures.upvalue4[1]
        humanoidRootPart = value0.FindFirstChild(value0, "HumanoidRootPart")
        if humanoidRootPart then
            value1 = humanoidRootPart.Position - captures.upvalue9[1].Position
            value2 = ENV.Vector3.new(value1.X, 0, value1.Z)
            if not (value2.Magnitude > 0.01) then
                value3 = captures.bodyVelocity[1]
                value3.Velocity = ENV.Vector3.zero
            else
                unit = value2.Unit
                value4 = ENV.math.clamp(captures.bodyPullSpeed[1], 5, 20)
                value5 = captures.bodyVelocity[1]
                value5.Velocity = ENV.Vector3.new(unit.X * value4, 0, unit.Z * value4)
                value5_2 = captures.upvalue9[1]
                value5_2.CFrame = ENV.CFrame.lookAt(captures.upvalue9[1].Position, ENV.Vector3.new(humanoidRootPart.Position.X, captures.upvalue9[1].Position.Y, humanoidRootPart.Position.Z))
            end
            if value1.Magnitude <= 2 then
                captures.bodyPullActive[1] = false
            end
            return
        end
        captures.bodyPullActive[1] = false
        return
    end
    if captures.upvalue6[1] then
        value0_2 = captures.upvalue6[1]
        value0_2.Disconnect(value0_2)
    end
    if captures.bodyVelocity[1] and captures.bodyVelocity[1].Parent then
        ENV.pcall(bind(Functions.setHumanoidMoveDirection, {bodyVelocity = captures.bodyVelocity}))
    end
    captures.restoreMovement[1](captures.upvalue5[1])
    captures.bodyPullActive[1] = false
end

        captures.refreshEspRenderer[1]()

end

            ENV.CreatePlayerTagDrawing(captures.upvalue1[1], captures.upvalue1[1].Parent.Name == "Killers" and captures.espConfig[1].EspKillerColor or captures.espConfig[1].EspSurvivorsColor, humanoidRootPart, humanoid)
            captures.updateAbilityOverlay[1](captures.upvalue1[1], humanoidRootPart, head, humanoid)

        return
    end
end

Functions.ensureEspGui = function(captures, a0, a1)
    local value2, value7, value12, uICorner, parent, parent_2, value14, parent_3

    for _, item59_2 in prepareIterator(ENV.ipairs(value2.GetChildren(value2))) do
        value7 = item59_2
        if value7.Name == "ForsakenAbilityESP" or value7.Name == "ForsakenDeviceESP" then
            for _, item60_2 in prepareIterator(ENV.ipairs(value7.GetDescendants(value7))) do
                value12 = item60_2
                if not (value12.Name == "IconFrame" and value12.IsA(value12, "Frame")) then
                    if value12.IsA(value12, "Frame") and value12.Parent and value12.Parent.Name == "ForsakenDeviceESP" then
                        value12.Size = ENV.UDim2.new(0, a1, 0, a1)
                    end
                else
                    value12.Size = ENV.UDim2.new(0, a0, 0, a0)
                    value12.Position = ENV.UDim2.new(0.5, -a0 / 2, 0, 20)
                    uICorner = value12.FindFirstChildOfClass(value12, "UICorner")
                    if uICorner then
                        uICorner.CornerRadius = ENV.UDim.new(0, ENV.math.max(4, ENV.math.floor(a0 * 0.18)))
                    end
                    parent = value12.Parent
                    if parent then
                        parent_2 = value12.Parent
                        value14 = parent_2.IsA(parent_2, "Frame")
                    else
                        value14 = parent
                    end
                    if value14 then
                        parent_3 = value12.Parent
                        parent_3.Size = ENV.UDim2.new(0, a0 + 6, 0, a0 + 28)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

Functions.getEspSetting = function(captures, a0, a1)
    if a0 ~= "Opt" then
        if a0 ~= "KillerEsp" then
            if a0 ~= "SurvEsp" then
                if a0 ~= "EspTextSize" then
                    if a0 ~= "EspIconSize" then
                        if a0 ~= "KillerColor" then
                            if a0 ~= "SurvColor" then
                                return nil
                            end
                            return captures.espConfig[1].EspSurvivorsColor
                        end
                        return captures.espConfig[1].EspKillerColor
                    end
                    return captures.espConfig[1].EspIconSize or 50
                end
                return captures.espConfig[1].EspTextSize or 14
            end
            return captures.espConfig[1].SurvivorsEsp == true
        end
        return captures.espConfig[1].KillerEsp == true
    end
    return (captures.espConfig[1].TagsOptions or {})[a1]
end

    return ENV.ESP_SET.dbg()
end

    for _, item69_2 in prepareIterator(ENV.ipairs(value0.GetChildren(value0))) do
        value6 = {item69_2}
        value7 = value6[1]
        value7_2 = value7.IsA(value7, "ScreenGui") and (value6[1].Name == "NergFK_TextTags" or value6[1].Name == "ForsakenDeviceESP")
        if value7_2 then
            value8 = value6[1]
            value7_3 = value8.GetAttribute(value8, "GEN") ~= 4
        else
            value7_3 = value7_2

    end
end

Functions.restoreEspSettings = function(captures)
    while true do
        if true then
            ENV.task.wait(5)
            ENV.pcall(bind(Functions.applyEspSettings, {espConfig = captures.espConfig, upvalue2 = captures.upvalue2, upvalue3 = captures.upvalue3, settings = captures.settings}))
            continue
        end
        break
    end
end


Functions.applyEspSettings = function(captures)
    local killerEsp, survivorsEsp
    if captures.espConfig[1].KillerEsp then
        ENV.pcall(ENV.killerEsp)
    end
    if captures.espConfig[1].SurvivorsEsp then
        ENV.pcall(ENV.survivorsEsp)
    end
    if captures.espConfig[1].KillerEsp ~= captures.upvalue2[1] or captures.espConfig[1].SurvivorsEsp ~= captures.upvalue3[1] then
        killerEsp = captures.espConfig[1].KillerEsp
        survivorsEsp = captures.espConfig[1].SurvivorsEsp
        captures.upvalue2[1] = killerEsp
        captures.upvalue3[1] = survivorsEsp
        ENV.pcall(bind(Functions.refreshEspSettings, {settings = captures.settings, espConfig = captures.espConfig}))
    end
end


Functions.refreshEspSettings = function(captures)
    if captures.settings[1].get("a720Telemetry", false) then
        ENV.print("[NERG] esp state: K=" .. ENV.tostring(captures.espConfig[1].KillerEsp) .. " S=" .. ENV.tostring(captures.espConfig[1].SurvivorsEsp) .. " (changed)")
    end
end

Functions.createHighlight = function(captures, a0, a1)
    local value0, value1, name, value4, refreshHighlight, value4_2, value5, childAdded, value3, external_Protected_HL, refreshHighlight_2, value4_3, value5_2, childAdded_2
    value0 = {a0}
    value1 = {a1}
    if not (not value0[1] or not value0[1].Parent) then
        name = captures.upvalue1[1].Name
        value4 = value0[1]
        if value4.GetAttribute(value4, "Username") ~= name then
            if not captures.upvalue2[1][value0[1]] then
                refreshHighlight = {
                    nil,
                    [1] = bind(Functions.refreshHighlight, {upvalue1 = value0, upvalue2 = captures.upvalue3, upvalue3 = value1, espConfig = captures.espConfig, settings = captures.settings}),
                }
                refreshHighlight[1]()
                value4_2 = captures.upvalue2[1]
                value5 = value0[1]
                childAdded = value0[1].ChildAdded
                value4_2[value5] = childAdded.Connect(childAdded, bind(Functions.onHighlightedDescendant, {upvalue1 = captures.upvalue3, refreshHighlight = refreshHighlight}))
                return
            end
            value3 = value0[1]
            external_Protected_HL = value3.FindFirstChild(value3, "External_Protected_HL")
            if not external_Protected_HL then
                refreshHighlight_2 = {
                    nil,
                    [1] = bind(Functions.refreshHighlight, {upvalue1 = value0, upvalue2 = captures.upvalue3, upvalue3 = value1, espConfig = captures.espConfig, settings = captures.settings}),
                }
                refreshHighlight_2[1]()
                value4_3 = captures.upvalue2[1]
                value5_2 = value0[1]
                childAdded_2 = value0[1].ChildAdded
                value4_3[value5_2] = childAdded_2.Connect(childAdded_2, bind(Functions.onHighlightedDescendant, {upvalue1 = captures.upvalue3, refreshHighlight = refreshHighlight_2}))
                return
            end
            external_Protected_HL.FillColor = value1[1]
            external_Protected_HL.OutlineColor = value1[1]
            return
        end
        return
    end
end


Functions.refreshHighlight = function(captures)
    local ipairs, value1, value4, value0, external_Protected_HL, highlight, value0_2
    ipairs = ENV.ipairs
    value1 = captures.upvalue1[1]
    for _, item71_2 in prepareIterator(ipairs(value1.GetDescendants(value1))) do
        value4 = item71_2
        if not (value4.IsA(value4, "Highlight") and value4.Name ~= captures.upvalue2[1]) then
            continue
        end
        value4.Destroy(value4)
    end
    value0 = captures.upvalue1[1]
    external_Protected_HL = value0.FindFirstChild(value0, "External_Protected_HL")
    if not external_Protected_HL then
        highlight = ENV.Instance.new("Highlight")
        highlight.Name = captures.upvalue2[1]
        highlight.Adornee = captures.upvalue1[1]
        highlight.Parent = captures.upvalue1[1]
        value0_2 = highlight
    else
        value0_2 = external_Protected_HL
    end
    value0_2.FillColor = captures.upvalue3[1]
    value0_2.OutlineColor = captures.upvalue3[1]
    value0_2.FillTransparency = captures.espConfig[1].HFillTransparency or 0.25
    value0_2.OutlineTransparency = captures.espConfig[1].DisableOutline or 0
    value0_2.DepthMode = ENV.Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
    ENV.pcall(bind(Functions.refreshHighlights, {settings = captures.settings, upvalue2 = captures.upvalue1, upvalue3 = captures.upvalue3}))
end


Functions.refreshHighlights = function(captures)
    if captures.settings[1].get("a720Telemetry", false) then
        ENV.print("[NERG] hl created: " .. ENV.tostring(captures.upvalue2[1].Name) .. " color=" .. ENV.tostring(captures.upvalue3[1]))
    end
end


Functions.onHighlightedDescendant = function(captures, a0)
    local value0, value1
    value0 = {a0}
    value1 = value0[1]

    end
end


Functions.onHighlightAdded = function(captures)

    if captures.upvalue1[1].Parent then
        value0 = captures.upvalue1[1]

end

Functions.scanKillerHighlights = function(captures)

            value8_2 = #value9.GetChildren(value9)
        else
            value8_2 = value8
        end
        print("[NERG] killerEsp: folder=" .. value4 .. " children=" .. (value8_2 or 0) .. " ON=" .. ENV.tostring(captures.espConfig[1].KillerEsp))
    end
end

Functions.createKillerHighlight = function(captures, a0)
    local humanoid, value2, value3, value2_2, model, value7, childAdded

    humanoid = a0.FindFirstChildOfClass(a0, "Humanoid")
    value2 = humanoid
    if value2 then
        value3 = captures.upvalue1[1]
        value2_2 = not value3.GetPlayerFromCharacter(value3, a0)
    else
        value2_2 = value2
    end
    if not (value2_2 and humanoid.WalkSpeed ~= 16 and humanoid.WalkSpeed ~= 26) then
        if not (a0.IsA(a0, "Folder") and ENV.string.sub(a0.Name, -7) == "Shadows") then
            if ENV.string.sub(a0.Name, -12) == "JohnDoeTrail" then
                model = ENV.Instance.new("Model")
                model.Name = "TrailESPModel"
                model.Parent = a0
                for _, item81_2 in prepareIterator(ENV.ipairs(a0.GetChildren(a0))) do
                    value7 = item81_2

                        value7.Transparency = 0

        end
    else
        ENV.createHighlight2(a0, captures.espConfig[1].EspKillerSupportColor)
    end
end

Functions.stopNoclip = function(captures)
    local value0, character, value5
    if captures.noclipConnection[1] then
        value0 = captures.noclipConnection[1]
        value0.Disconnect(value0)
        captures.noclipConnection[1] = nil
    end
    character = captures.localPlayer[1].Character
    if character then
        for _, item87_2 in prepareIterator(ENV.ipairs(character.GetDescendants(character))) do
            value5 = item87_2
            if value5.IsA(value5, "BasePart") then
                value5.CanCollide = true
            end
        end
    end
end


Functions.startNoclip = function(captures)
    local value0, stepped
    if captures.localPlayer[1].Character then
        if captures.noclipConnection[1] then
            value0 = captures.noclipConnection[1]
            value0.Disconnect(value0)
        end
        stepped = captures.runService[1].Stepped
        captures.noclipConnection[1] = stepped.Connect(stepped, bind(Functions.stepNoclip, {
            noclipEnabled = captures.noclipEnabled,
            noclipConnection = captures.noclipConnection,
            noclipOnlyWhenBlockReady = captures.noclipOnlyWhenBlockReady,
            isBlockReady = captures.isBlockReady,
            localPlayer = captures.localPlayer,
        }))
        return
    end
end


Functions.stepNoclip = function(captures)
    local ipairs, character, value4, value0
    if captures.noclipEnabled[1] then
        if not (captures.noclipOnlyWhenBlockReady[1] and not captures.isBlockReady[1]()) then
            if captures.localPlayer[1].Character then
                ipairs = ENV.ipairs
                character = captures.localPlayer[1].Character
                for _, item88_2 in prepareIterator(ipairs(character.GetDescendants(character))) do
                    value4 = item88_2
                    if value4.IsA(value4, "BasePart") then
                        value4.CanCollide = false
                    end
                end
                return
            end
            return
        end
        return
    end
    value0 = captures.noclipConnection[1]
    value0.Disconnect(value0)
    captures.noclipConnection[1] = nil
end


Functions.cancelChargeTween = function(captures, a0)
    local value0, value1, value2, value1_2, value2_2, value3, value3_2
    value0 = {a0}
    value1 = not value0[1]
    if not value1 then
        value2 = value0[1]
        value1_2 = not value2.IsA(value2, "NumberValue")
    else
        value1_2 = value1
    end
    value2_2 = {nil}
    if not value1_2 then
        if not (value0[1].Value <= 1.01) then
            ENV.pcall(bind(Functions.cancelChargePartTween, {upvalue1 = value0, tweenService = captures.tweenService}))
            value2_2[1] = nil
            value3 = value0[1]
            value3_2 = value3.GetPropertyChangedSignal(value3, "Value")
            value2_2[1] = value3_2.Connect(value3_2, bind(Functions.cancelChargeTweenTask, {chargeActive = captures.chargeActive, chargeSpeedCapEnabled = captures.chargeSpeedCapEnabled, upvalue3 = value2_2, upvalue4 = value0}))
            ENV.table.insert(captures.chargeTweens[1], value2_2[1])
            return
        end
        return
    end
end


Functions.cancelChargePartTween = function(captures)
    local value0, value0_2
    captures.upvalue1[1].Value = 1
    value0 = captures.tweenService[1]
    value0_2 = value0.Create(value0, captures.upvalue1[1], ENV.TweenInfo.new(0), {Value = 1})
    value0_2.Play(value0_2)
end


Functions.cancelChargeTweenTask = function(captures)
    if not (not captures.chargeActive[1] or not captures.chargeSpeedCapEnabled[1]) then
        if captures.upvalue4[1].Value > 1 then
            ENV.pcall(bind(Functions.cancelChargeInnerTask, {upvalue1 = captures.upvalue4}))
        end
        return
    end
    ENV.pcall(bind(Functions.cancelChargeTask, {upvalue1 = captures.upvalue3}))
end


Functions.cancelChargeTask = function(captures)
    local value0
    value0 = captures.upvalue1[1]
    value0.Disconnect(value0)
end


Functions.cancelChargeInnerTask = function(captures)
    captures.upvalue1[1].Value = 1
end


Functions.cancelPunchTween = function(captures, a0)
    local value0, value1, value2, value1_2, value2_2, value3, value3_2
    value0 = {a0}
    value1 = not value0[1]
    if not value1 then
        value2 = value0[1]
        value1_2 = not value2.IsA(value2, "NumberValue")
    else
        value1_2 = value1
    end
    value2_2 = {nil}
    if not value1_2 then
        if not (value0[1].Value > 1.01) then
            ENV.pcall(bind(Functions.cancelPunchPartTween, {upvalue1 = value0, tweenService = captures.tweenService}))
            value2_2[1] = nil
            value3 = value0[1]
            value3_2 = value3.GetPropertyChangedSignal(value3, "Value")
            value2_2[1] = value3_2.Connect(value3_2, bind(Functions.cancelPunchTweenTask, {punchMobilityEnabled = captures.punchMobilityEnabled, upvalue2 = value2_2, upvalue3 = value0}))
            ENV.table.insert(captures.punchTweens[1], value2_2[1])
            return
        end
        return
    end
end


Functions.cancelPunchPartTween = function(captures)
    local value0, value0_2
    captures.upvalue1[1].Value = 1
    value0 = captures.tweenService[1]
    value0_2 = value0.Create(value0, captures.upvalue1[1], ENV.TweenInfo.new(0), {Value = 1})

Functions.cancelPunchTweenTask = function(captures)
    if captures.punchMobilityEnabled[1] then
        if captures.upvalue3[1].Value < 1 then
            ENV.pcall(bind(Functions.cancelPunchInnerTask, {upvalue1 = captures.upvalue3}))
        end
        return
    end
    ENV.pcall(bind(Functions.cancelPunchTask, {upvalue1 = captures.upvalue2}))
end


Functions.cancelPunchTask = function(captures)
    local value0
    value0 = captures.upvalue1[1]
    value0.Disconnect(value0)
end


Functions.cancelPunchInnerTask = function(captures)
    captures.upvalue1[1].Value = 1
end


Functions.startPunchMobility = function(captures)
    local character, value5, descendantAdded, value1
    captures.cleanupPunchMobility[1]()
    character = captures.localPlayer[1].Character
    if character then
        for _, item89_2 in prepareIterator(ENV.ipairs(character.GetDescendants(character))) do
            value5 = item89_2
            if value5.IsA(value5, "NumberValue") then
                captures.cancelPunchTween[1](value5)
            end
        end
        descendantAdded = character.DescendantAdded
        captures.punchMobilityConnection[1] = descendantAdded.Connect(descendantAdded, bind(Functions.onPunchMobilityAnimation, {
            punchMobilityEnabled = captures.punchMobilityEnabled,
            lungeBoostEnabled = captures.lungeBoostEnabled,
            punchMobilityTask = captures.punchMobilityTask,
            startLungeBoost = captures.startLungeBoost,
            cancelPunchTween = captures.cancelPunchTween,
        }))
        value1 = captures.runService[1]
        value1.BindToRenderStep(value1, "PunchMobility", ENV.Enum.RenderPriority.Character.Value + 10, bind(Functions.onPunchMobilityCharacter, {punchMobilityActive = captures.punchMobilityActive, getLocalCharacter = captures.getLocalCharacter}))
        return
    end
end


Functions.onPunchMobilityAnimation = function(captures, a0)
    local value0, value1
    value0 = {a0}
    if captures.punchMobilityEnabled[1] then
        value1 = value0[1]
        if value1.IsA(value1, "NumberValue") then
            ENV.task.defer(bind(Functions.attachPunchMobilityAnimator, {
                upvalue1 = value0,
                lungeBoostEnabled = captures.lungeBoostEnabled,
                punchMobilityTask = captures.punchMobilityTask,
                startLungeBoost = captures.startLungeBoost,
                cancelPunchTween = captures.cancelPunchTween,
            }))
        end
        return
    end
end


Functions.attachPunchMobilityAnimator = function(captures)
    if captures.upvalue1[1].Name == "PunchAbility" then
        if captures.lungeBoostEnabled[1] then
            if captures.punchMobilityTask[1] then
                ENV.task.cancel(captures.punchMobilityTask[1])
            end
            captures.punchMobilityTask[1] = ENV.task.delay(0.7, bind(Functions.onPunchMobilityAnimatorAdded, {punchMobilityTask = captures.punchMobilityTask, startLungeBoost = captures.startLungeBoost}))
        end
    end
    captures.cancelPunchTween[1](captures.upvalue1[1])
end


Functions.onPunchMobilityAnimatorAdded = function(captures)
    captures.punchMobilityTask[1] = nil
    captures.startLungeBoost[1]()
end


Functions.onPunchMobilityCharacter = function(captures)
    local value0
    if not captures.punchMobilityActive[1] then
        value0 = captures.getLocalCharacter[1]()
        if value0 then
            if value0.WalkSpeed < 12 then
                value0.WalkSpeed = 12
            end
            return
        end
        return
    end
end


Functions.startLungeBoost = function(captures)
    local value0, value1, value2, value3, lookVector, value5
    value0 = {nil}
    value1 = {nil}
    value2 = {nil}
    if captures.lungeBoostEnabled[1] then
        if not captures.punchMobilityActive[1] then
            value3 = captures.getLocalRoot[1]()
            if value3 then
                lookVector = value3.CFrame.LookVector
                value0[1] = ENV.Vector3.new(lookVector.X, 0, lookVector.Z)
                if value0[1].Magnitude ~= 0 then
                    value0[1] = value0[1].Unit
                    captures.punchMobilityActive[1] = true
                    value1[1] = 40 * captures.lungeMultiplier[1]
                    value2[1] = 0
                    value5 = captures.runService[1]
                    value5.BindToRenderStep(value5, "LungeBoost", ENV.Enum.RenderPriority.Character.Value + 20, bind(Functions.stepLungeBoost, {
                        upvalue1 = value2,
                        getLocalRoot = captures.getLocalRoot,
                        runService = captures.runService,
                        punchMobilityActive = captures.punchMobilityActive,
                        upvalue5 = value0,
                        upvalue6 = value1,
                    }))
                    return
                end
                return
            end
            return
        end
        return
    end
end


Functions.stepLungeBoost = function(captures, a0)
    local value1
    captures.upvalue1[1] = captures.upvalue1[1] + a0
    value1 = captures.getLocalRoot[1]()
    if not (not value1 or captures.upvalue1[1] >= 0.3) then
        value1.AssemblyLinearVelocity = captures.upvalue5[1] * (captures.upvalue6[1] * (1 - captures.upvalue1[1] / 0.3)) + ENV.Vector3.new(0, value1.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, 0)
        return
    end
    ENV.pcall(bind(Functions.restoreLungeAutoRotate, {runService = captures.runService}))
    captures.punchMobilityActive[1] = false
end


Functions.restoreLungeAutoRotate = function(captures)
    local value0
    value0 = captures.runService[1]
    value0.UnbindFromRenderStep(value0, "LungeBoost")
end


Functions.attachChargeCharacter = function(captures)
    local character, descendantAdded, value1
    captures.stopCharge[1]()
    character = captures.localPlayer[1].Character
    if character then
        for _, item90_2 in prepareIterator(ENV.ipairs(character.GetDescendants(character))) do
            captures.cancelChargeTween[1](item90_2)
        end
        descendantAdded = character.DescendantAdded

Functions.updateVelocityParts = function(captures, a0)
    local value4, value1, players, players_2, killers, value6, humanoidRootPart, value10, value10_2, value10_3, vel, lookVector, prevLook, value12, value13, value14, prevLook_2, value15, value16
    for item92_1 in prepareIterator(ENV.pairs(captures.velocityVisuals[1])) do
        value4 = item92_1
        if not value4.Parent then
            captures.velocityVisuals[1][value4] = nil
        end
    end
    value1 = captures.workspace[1]
    players = value1.FindFirstChild(value1, "Players")
    if players then
        players_2 = captures.workspace[1].Players
        killers = players_2.FindFirstChild(players_2, "Killers")
    else
        killers = players
    end
    if killers then
        for _, item93_2 in prepareIterator(ENV.ipairs(killers.GetChildren(killers))) do
            value6 = item93_2
            humanoidRootPart = value6.FindFirstChild(value6, "HumanoidRootPart")
            if humanoidRootPart then
                value10 = captures.velocityVisuals[1][value6]
                if not value10 then
                    value10_2 = {prevPos = humanoidRootPart.Position, prevLook = humanoidRootPart.CFrame.LookVector, vel = ENV.Vector3.new(), angVel = 0}
                else
                    value10_2 = value10
                end
                value10_3 = ENV.math.max(a0, 0.000001)
                vel = value10_2.vel
                value10_2.vel = vel.Lerp(vel, (humanoidRootPart.Position - value10_2.prevPos) / value10_3, 0.22)
                lookVector = humanoidRootPart.CFrame.LookVector
                prevLook = value10_2.prevLook
                value12 = prevLook.Dot(prevLook, lookVector)
                value13 = ENV.math.clamp(value12, -1, 1)
                value14 = ENV.math.acos(value13)
                prevLook_2 = value10_2.prevLook
                value15 = prevLook_2.Cross(prevLook_2, lookVector)
                value16 = ENV.math.max(a0, 0.000001)
                value10_2.angVel = value10_2.angVel * 0.78 + value14 / value16 * (value15.Y >= 0 and 1 or -1) * 0.22
                value10_2.prevPos = humanoidRootPart.Position
                value10_2.prevLook = lookVector
                captures.velocityVisuals[1][value6] = value10_2
            end
        end
        return
    end
end


Functions.stepPunchAim = function(captures)
    local value2, value3, value0, value0_2, value0_3, value0_4, players, players_2, killers, value1, huge, value4, value5, value6, value4_2, value5_2, value6_2, humanoidRootPart, magnitude, value4_3, assemblyLinearVelocity, value6_3, value7
    if captures.punchAiming[1] and (not captures.aimPunchActive[1] or not captures.localHumanoid[1] or not captures.localRoot[1] or ENV.tick() - captures.punchAimStartedAt[1] > captures.aimPunchDuration[1]) then
        captures.punchAiming[1] = false
        captures.restoreMovement[1](captures.localHumanoid[1])
        if ENV.walkSpeed3 then
            value0 = captures.localHumanoid[1]
            value0.WalkSpeed = ENV.walkSpeed3
            value0_2 = captures.localHumanoid[1]
            value0_2.JumpPower = ENV.jumpPower
            ENV.walkSpeed3 = nil
            ENV.jumpPower = nil
            ENV.autoRotate2 = nil
        end
    end
    if captures.aimPunchActive[1] and captures.localHumanoid[1] and captures.localRoot[1] and captures.punchAiming[1] and ENV.tick() - captures.punchAimStartedAt[1] <= captures.aimPunchDuration[1] then
        if not ENV.walkSpeed3 then
            ENV.walkSpeed3 = captures.localHumanoid[1].WalkSpeed
            ENV.jumpPower = captures.localHumanoid[1].JumpPower
        end
        captures.lockMovement[1](captures.localHumanoid[1])
        captures.localHumanoid[1].AutoRotate = false
        value0_3 = captures.localRoot[1]
        value0_3.AssemblyAngularVelocity = ENV.Vector3.zero
        value0_4 = captures.workspace[1]
        players = value0_4.FindFirstChild(value0_4, "Players")
        if players then
            players_2 = captures.workspace[1].Players
            killers = players_2.FindFirstChild(players_2, "Killers")
        else
            killers = players
        end
        value1 = killers
        if value1 and captures.localRoot[1] then
            huge = ENV.math.huge
            value4, value5, value6 = ENV.ipairs(value1.GetChildren(value1))
            value4_2, value5_2, value6_2 = prepareIterator(value4, value5, value6)
            value2 = huge
            value3 = nil
            for _, item94_2 in value4_2, value5_2, value6_2 do
                humanoidRootPart = item94_2.FindFirstChild(item94_2, "HumanoidRootPart")
                if humanoidRootPart then
                    magnitude = (humanoidRootPart.Position - captures.localRoot[1].Position).Magnitude
                    if magnitude < value2 then
                        value2 = magnitude
                        value3 = humanoidRootPart
                    end
                end
            end
            if value3 then
                value4_3 = captures.punchPrediction[1]
                assemblyLinearVelocity = value3.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                value6_3 = ((assemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude > 0.5 and value3.Position + assemblyLinearVelocity * (value4_3 / 60) or value3.Position) - captures.localRoot[1].Position) * ENV.Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)
                if value6_3.Magnitude > 0.01 then
                    value7 = captures.localRoot[1]
                    value7.CFrame = ENV.CFrame.new(captures.localRoot[1].Position, captures.localRoot[1].Position + value6_3.Unit)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    captures.updateTriggerVisual[1]()
end

end


Functions.assignKillerColorConfig = function(captures, value)
    local value1, value1_2, value2, value3, value1_3, value2_2, value3_2
    value1 = ENV.tostring(value)
    value1_2, value2, value3 = value1.match(value1, "^(%d+),(%d+),(%d+)$")
    value1_3 = {value1_2}
    value2_2 = {value2}
    value3_2 = {value3}
    if value1_3[1] then
        ENV.pcall(bind(Functions.applyKillerColorConfig, {upvalue1 = value1_3, upvalue2 = value2_2, upvalue3 = value3_2}))
    end
end

Functions.syncConfigControls = function(captures)

        value6 = {value5[1][6] and captures.controls[1][value5[1][6]]}
        if value6[1] then
            ENV.pcall(bind(Functions.setControlValue, {upvalue1 = value6, upvalue2 = value5}))
        end
    end
end


Functions.setControlValue = function(captures)

    value0 = captures.upvalue1[1]
    value0.Set(value0, captures.upvalue2[1][3]())
end

end

Functions.createConfigExportButton = function(captures)
    local cfgTab
    cfgTab = ENV.cfgTab
    cfgTab.Button(cfgTab, {
        Title = "Generate config (copy code)",
        Justify = "Center",
        Callback = bind(Functions.exportConfig, {configEntries_2 = captures.configEntries_2, httpService = captures.httpService, notify = captures.notify}),
    })
end


Functions.exportConfig = function(captures)
    local value0, value6, value7, value1, value2, value2_2, value3, value4, value5
    value0 = {{}}
    for _, item96_2 in prepareIterator(ENV.ipairs(captures.configEntries_2[1])) do
        value6 = value0[1]
        value7 = item96_2[2]
        value6[value7] = item96_2[3]()
    end
    value1, value2 = ENV.pcall(bind(Functions.encodeConfigJson, {httpService = captures.httpService, upvalue2 = value0}))
    value2_2 = {value2}
    if not (not value1 or not value2_2[1]) then
        value3, value4 = ENV.pcall(bind(Functions.encodeConfigBase64, {httpService = captures.httpService, upvalue2 = value2_2}))
        value5 = "VP1|" .. (value3 and value4 or value2_2[1])
        if ENV.setclipboard then
            ENV.pcall(ENV.setclipboard, value5)
        end
        captures.notify[1]("copied config code (" .. #captures.configEntries_2[1] .. " keys) — stash it somewhere safe")
        return
    end
    captures.notify[1]("generate failed (JSON)")
end


Functions.encodeConfigJson = function(captures)
    local value0
    value0 = captures.httpService[1]
    return value0.JSONEncode(value0, captures.upvalue2[1])
end


Functions.encodeConfigBase64 = function(captures)
    local value0
    value0 = captures.httpService[1]
    return value0.Base64Encode(value0, captures.upvalue2[1])
end

Functions.resetConfig = function(captures)
    for _, item97_2 in prepareIterator(ENV.ipairs(captures.configEntries_2[1])) do
        captures.settings[1].set(item97_2[2], item97_2[5])
        item97_2[4](item97_2[5])
    end
    captures.syncConfigControls[1]()
    captures.notify[1]("reset to defaults — squeaky clean")
end


Functions.createConfigImportInput = function(captures)
    local value0, cfgTab, cfgTab_2
    value0 = {""}
    cfgTab = ENV.cfgTab

    })
    cfgTab_2 = ENV.cfgTab
    cfgTab_2.Button(cfgTab_2, {
        Title = "Load config code",
        Justify = "Center",
        Callback = bind(Functions.importConfig, {
            upvalue1 = value0,
            notify = captures.notify,
            httpService = captures.httpService,
            configEntries_2 = captures.configEntries_2,
            settings = captures.settings,
            syncConfigControls = captures.syncConfigControls,
        }),
    })
end


Functions.setConfigImportText = function(captures, a0)
    captures.upvalue1[1] = ENV.tostring(a0 or "")
end


Functions.importConfig = function(captures)
    local value5, value0, value1, value1_2, value2, value1_3, value1_4, value2_2, value4, value3, value4_2, value3_2, value4_3, value6, value7, value8, value6_2, value7_2, value8_2, value10, value11
    value0 = {captures.upvalue1[1]}
    if (not value0[1] or value0[1] == "") and ENV.getclipboard then
        ENV.pcall(bind(Functions.readImportClipboard, {upvalue1 = value0}))
    end
    value1 = ENV.tostring(value0[1] or "")
    value1_2 = value1.gsub(value1, "^%s+", "")
    value0[1] = value1_2.gsub(value1_2, "%s+$", "")
    value2 = value0[1]
    if value2.sub(value2, 1, 4) == "VP1|" then
        value1_3 = value0[1]
        value1_4 = {(value1_3.sub(value1_3, 5))}
        value2_2 = {value1_4[1]}
        value4 = value1_4[1]
        if not value4.find(value4, "{", 1, true) then
            value3, value4_2 = ENV.pcall(bind(Functions.decodeConfigBase64, {httpService = captures.httpService, upvalue2 = value1_4}))
            if value3 and value4_2 then
                value2_2[1] = value4_2
            end
        end
        value3_2, value4_3 = ENV.pcall(bind(Functions.decodeConfigJson, {httpService = captures.httpService, upvalue2 = value2_2}))
        if not (not value3_2 or ENV.type(value4_3) ~= "table") then
            value6, value7, value8 = ENV.ipairs(captures.configEntries_2[1])
            value6_2, value7_2, value8_2 = prepareIterator(value6, value7, value8)
            value5 = 0
            for _, item98_2 in value6_2, value7_2, value8_2 do
                value10 = item98_2
                if value4_3[value10[2]] ~= nil then
                    captures.settings[1].set(value10[2], value4_3[value10[2]])
                    value10[4](value4_3[value10[2]])
                    value11 = value5 + 1
                    value5 = value11
                end
            end
            captures.syncConfigControls[1]()
            captures.notify[1]("loaded " .. value5 .. " keys — live & cooking")
            return
        end
        captures.notify[1]("couldn't decode that code")
        return
    end
    captures.notify[1]("invalid code (missing VP1| — old code or clipboard junk)")
end


Functions.readImportClipboard = function(captures)
    captures.upvalue1[1] = ENV.getclipboard()
end


Functions.decodeConfigBase64 = function(captures)
    local value0
    value0 = captures.httpService[1]
    return value0.Base64Decode(value0, captures.upvalue2[1])
end


Functions.decodeConfigJson = function(captures)
    local value0
    value0 = captures.httpService[1]
    return value0.JSONDecode(value0, captures.upvalue2[1])
end


Functions.onToggleKey = function(captures, input, processed)
    if not processed then
        if input.KeyCode == ENV.Enum.KeyCode.Minus and captures.window[1] then
            ENV.pcall(bind(Functions.toggleWindow, {window = captures.window, settings = captures.settings}))
        end
        return
    end
end


Functions.toggleWindow = function(captures)
    local value0, value0_2
    if not captures.window[1].Closed then
        value0 = captures.window[1]
        value0.Close(value0)
        if captures.settings[1].get("a720Telemetry", false) then
            ENV.print("[NERG] ui hidden ('-' to bring back)")
        end
    else
        value0_2 = captures.window[1]
        value0_2.Open(value0_2)
        if captures.settings[1].get("a720Telemetry", false) then
            ENV.print("[NERG] ui back on screen")
        end
    end
end


Functions.shutdown = function(captures, reason)
    local value6
    captures.alive[1] = false
    captures.punchAiming[1] = false
    if ENV.walkSpeed3 then
        ENV.pcall(bind(Functions.restoreHumanoidRotation, {localHumanoid = captures.localHumanoid}))
        ENV.walkSpeed3 = nil
        ENV.jumpPower = nil
        ENV.autoRotate2 = nil
    end
    ENV.chargeAim = false
    ENV.chargeCamSteer = false
    ENV.autoCharge = false
    ENV.pcall(bind(Functions.restoreCharacterMovement, {localPlayer = captures.localPlayer}))
    captures.autoBlockAudioOn[1] = false
    captures.autoPunchOn[1] = false
    captures.aimPunchActive[1] = false
    captures.bodyPullEnabled[1] = false
    captures.showVisionRange[1] = false
    ENV.pcall(captures.clearVisuals[1])
    for _, item99_2 in prepareIterator(ENV.pairs(ENV.animEarConns)) do
        value6 = {item99_2}
        ENV.pcall(bind(Functions.disconnectConnection, {upvalue1 = value6}))
    end
    ENV.pcall(bind(Functions.destroyVisualFolder, {visualFolder = captures.visualFolder}))
    ENV.pcall(bind(Functions.destroyWindow, {window = captures.window}))
    ENV.pcall(bind(Functions.disconnectAudioListener, {}))
    ENV.pcall(bind(Functions.disconnectUiKey, {}))
    ENV.pcall(bind(Functions.disconnectNoclip, {noclipConnection = captures.noclipConnection}))
    ENV.print("[NERG] old instance fully shut down (auto-cleanup: " .. ENV.tostring(reason) .. ")")
end


Functions.restoreHumanoidRotation = function(captures)
    local value0
    if captures.localHumanoid[1] then
        value0 = captures.localHumanoid[1]
        value0.AutoRotate = ENV.autoRotate2
    end
end


Functions.restoreCharacterMovement = function(captures)
    local character, value1
    character = captures.localPlayer[1].Character
    value1 = {character and character.FindFirstChildOfClass(character, "Humanoid")}
    if value1[1] then
        value1[1].AutoRotate = true
        ENV.pcall(bind(Functions.clearHumanoidMovement, {upvalue1 = value1}))
    end
end


Functions.clearHumanoidMovement = function(captures)
    local value0
    value0 = captures.upvalue1[1]
    value0.Move(value0, ENV.Vector3.zero)
end


Functions.disconnectConnection = function(captures)
    local value0
    value0 = captures.upvalue1[1]
    value0.Disconnect(value0)
end


Functions.destroyVisualFolder = function(captures)
    local value0
    value0 = captures.visualFolder[1]
    value0.Destroy(value0)
end


Functions.destroyWindow = function(captures)
    local value0
    value0 = captures.window[1]
    value0.Destroy(value0)
end


Functions.disconnectAudioListener = function(captures)
    local a655EarConn
    if ENV.a655EarConn then
        a655EarConn = ENV.a655EarConn
        a655EarConn.Disconnect(a655EarConn)
        ENV.a655EarConn = nil
    end
end


Functions.disconnectUiKey = function(captures)
    local a665UiKey
    if ENV.a665UiKey then
        a665UiKey = ENV.a665UiKey
        a665UiKey.Disconnect(a665UiKey)
        ENV.a665UiKey = nil
    end
end


Functions.disconnectNoclip = function(captures)
    local value0
    if captures.noclipConnection[1] then
        value0 = captures.noclipConnection[1]
        value0.Disconnect(value0)
        captures.noclipConnection[1] = nil
    end
end

return Functions.initialize({}, ...)
